# DOC_TOUCH_LOG.md

> **Purpose:** Lightweight tracking log for doc inventory files that must be updated when a feature ships. Codex (or any contributor) appends one row per feature delivery. Reviewers scan this log to confirm inventory drift did not sneak in.

---

## How to use this log

When you ship a chunk that adds, changes, or removes something visible to the operator (a new DOM element, a new export, a new route, a new smoke test, a new doc page):

1. Decide which inventory files are in scope for this change (see checklist below).
2. Update each applicable inventory file **in the same chunk** as the feature.
3. Append one row to the `## Entries` table below: date, chunk title, and which inventories were touched or confirmed unchanged.

If an inventory file is genuinely not affected, mark it **n/a** — do not leave the cell blank.

---

## Inventory files checklist

| File | What it tracks | Update when… |
|---|---|---|
| `Docs/inventories/WEBVIEW_DOM_ID_INVENTORY.md` | Every DOM id in the WebView static assets | A new `id=` attribute is added or removed |
| `Docs/inventories/WEBVIEW_GLOBAL_EXPORT_INVENTORY.md` | Every `window.*` export from WebView JS | A new global is exposed or an old one is removed |
| `Docs/inventories/API_ROUTE_INVENTORY.md` | Every local API route (GET + POST) | A new route is added or an existing route changes method/auth |
| `Docs/testing/BROWSER_SMOKE_DOES_NOT_MUTATE_MATRIX.md` | Which mutation routes each browser smoke proves are not called | A new browser smoke is added, or a smoke's POST-block assertions change |
| `Docs/testing/TEST_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md` | Which behaviours are covered by which test files | A new test file is added or a significant new scenario is covered |
| `Docs/DOCS_INDEX.md` | Master index of all docs in the workspace | A new doc is created, archived, or renamed |
| `Docs/ARCHIVED_MD_INDEX.md` | Index of archived docs | A doc is moved to `Docs/archive/` |
| `Docs/architecture/TAURI_WEBVIEW_PARITY_MATRIX.md` | Tk vs WebView feature parity row-by-row | A parity gap is closed or a new Tk feature is identified |
| `Docs/testing/VALIDATION_LADDER_RUNBOOK.md` | The ordered smoke/test ladder rungs | A new smoke is added to a rung, or rung counts change |
| `Docs/REMEDIATION_CHANGELOG.md` | Chronological chunk log | Every chunk — append a dated H2 entry |

---

## Rules

- **One row per delivered chunk**, not one row per file touched.
- **Date format:** `YYYY-MM-DD`.
- **Chunk title:** Match the dated H2 heading used in `Docs/REMEDIATION_CHANGELOG.md`.
- If a chunk touches no inventory files at all (e.g., pure documentation pass), write `doc-only — no inventory files affected` in the Inventories column.
- Do not use this log to defer inventory updates. The inventory file must be updated before appending here.

| 2026-05-18 | Dashboard queue tile always shows 0/0 — fix wiring to queue snapshot | n/a (JS-only fix — no new IDs, routes, or exports) | `WEBVIEW_DOM_ID_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `WEBVIEW_GLOBAL_EXPORT_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `API_ROUTE_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `BROWSER_SMOKE_DOES_NOT_MUTATE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TEST_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TAURI_WEBVIEW_PARITY_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `VALIDATION_LADDER_RUNBOOK.md` (n/a), `DOCS_INDEX.md` (n/a), `ARCHIVED_MD_INDEX.md` (n/a), `REMEDIATION_CHANGELOG.md` (n/a) | **Root cause:** `renderSnapshot` set `queue-count` from `snapshot.counts.queue_index / queue_total`, which are `CurrentQueueIndex / CurrentQueueTotal` from the pipeline progress JSON. These are 0/0 whenever the pipeline is idle. The actual queue size (`runnable_count / rows.length`) from the `/api/queue` snapshot was never wired to the tile. **Fix:** `app.js` — removed `setText("queue-count", ...)` from `renderSnapshot` (left a comment explaining why); added `setText("queue-count", \`${runnable} / ${rows.length}\`)` to `renderHomeQueueSnapshot` which runs second in every poll cycle using real queue snapshot data. Tile now shows runnable/total from the queue file at all times. |
| 2026-05-18 | Pipeline bug fixes — Trigun TV parse failure + NVENC probe minimum dimension | `REMEDIATION_CHANGELOG.md` (n/a — pipeline-only fix) | `WEBVIEW_DOM_ID_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `WEBVIEW_GLOBAL_EXPORT_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `API_ROUTE_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `BROWSER_SMOKE_DOES_NOT_MUTATE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TEST_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TAURI_WEBVIEW_PARITY_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `VALIDATION_LADDER_RUNBOOK.md` (n/a), `DOCS_INDEX.md` (n/a), `ARCHIVED_MD_INDEX.md` (n/a) | **Bug 1 — TV parse failure for currency-title episode** (`Naming.ps1` `Get-TVLooseBareEpisodeNumber`): added two regex strips before numeric candidate scan — `'[$£€¥]\s*\d[\d,.]*'` (currency amounts) and `'\b\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+\b'` (large comma-grouped numbers). Root cause: `[Anime Time] Trigun - 001 - The $60,000,000,000 Man.mkv` produced two numeric candidates (1 from `001` and 60 from `$60`), causing the single-unique-number heuristic to return `$null` → `IsReliable=false` → permanent `SOURCE_TV_PARSE_FAILED` failure. Fix restores single-candidate behaviour; all 25 other Trigun episodes were already passing. Failure marker `86fe4e6c...` deleted from `E:\Videos\Scratch\State\Failures\Markers\` so pipeline will retry on next run. **Bug 2 — NVENC probe always fails with exit -22 on RTX 5080** (`EncodePolicy.ps1` `Test-NvencAvailable`): changed lavfi probe resolution from `64x64` to `256x144`. Root cause: RTX 5080 (Blackwell) requires minimum frame dimensions above 128×128 for `hevc_nvenc`; 64×64 caused `InitializeEncoder failed: invalid param (8): Frame dimensions are less than the minimum supported value`. 144×144 is the observed minimum; 256×144 chosen as safe headroom. Fix restores GPU-first encode path for all files requiring encode; all 277 files in current runs were remuxes so no files were actually blocked, but future encode candidates would have fallen back to CPU. |
| 2026-05-18 | S50 Layout Manager — edge case fixes #1 / #6 / #8 / #10 | n/a (JS + CSS only — no new IDs or routes) | `WEBVIEW_DOM_ID_INVENTORY.md` (n/a — `layout-reset-warning` is dynamically injected, not a static id), `WEBVIEW_GLOBAL_EXPORT_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `API_ROUTE_INVENTORY.md` (n/a), `BROWSER_SMOKE_DOES_NOT_MUTATE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TEST_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `TAURI_WEBVIEW_PARITY_MATRIX.md` (n/a), `VALIDATION_LADDER_RUNBOOK.md` (n/a), `DOCS_INDEX.md` (n/a), `ARCHIVED_MD_INDEX.md` (n/a), `REMEDIATION_CHANGELOG.md` (n/a) | **Fix #1 (duplicate key collision):** `_initPageLayout` now tracks seen keys per page in `_keySeen Map`; second panel with identical h2 gets `-2` suffix, third gets `-3`, etc. Prevents silent localStorage collision. **Fix #6 + #10 (reset button):** `window.confirm()` removed entirely. Reset button now uses two-step inline confirm: first click arms it (label → "Confirm Reset?", pulsing border, 3-s auto-disarm); second click executes. If `closeReadinessRequiresWarning()` returns true (unsaved Rename/Settings/Launch state), a third step is required ("Reset Anyway?") with an inline warning note injected below the topbar. Fully safe in Tauri WebView where `confirm()` is suppressed. **Fix #8 (hidden panel height):** In customize mode, `data-panel-hidden` panels now collapse to customize-bar-only height (`> *:not(.panel-customize-bar) { display:none!important }`); bar background turns red, panel border becomes dashed red. Eliminates the ghost-height confusion where hidden panels looked nearly identical to visible ones. **CSS additions:** `.layout-reset-warning-note` (inline warning below topbar), `#reset-layout-btn[data-state="armed/forced"]` pulsing animation, `layout-reset-pulse` keyframes. `LAYOUT_MANAGER_EDGE_CASES.md` created in `Docs/ui/` tracking all 10 issues with resolution status. |
